def analyze_institutional_conflict(data: dict) -> dict:
    """
    data keys:
      securities_lending_add: ?祆?憓???撘蛛?
      foreign_net_buy: 憭??曇疏瘛刻眺頞?撘蛛?

    """

    fb = data['foreign_net_buy']
    pc = data['price_change_pct']
    
    # 銵??
    conflict_ratio = abs(sl) / (abs(fb) + 1)
    
    if fb > 0 and sl > 0:
        if pc > 0:
            return {"signal": "?? 憭?憭征?? - 撠?蝑嚗隅?Ｖ???,

                    "conflict": "璆菟?", "action": "雓寞?"}

        return {"signal": "? 憭?憭折?頠征 - 撘瑕???閮?", 
                "conflict": "雿?, "action": "蝛扔??"}

def margin_risk_assessment(margin_balance: float, market_value: float) -> dict:
    """
    margin_balance: ??擗?嚗?嚗?
    market_value: ?∠巨撣潘???
    """
    maintenance_rate = market_value / margin_balance * 100
    
    levels = {
        "rate": round(maintenance_rate, 1),
        "zone": "",
        "risk": "",
        "action": ""
    }
    
    if maintenance_rate < 120:
        levels["zone"] = "? 餈賜像?嚗? 120%嚗?

        levels["action"] = "?踹???嚗?敺????脣"
    elif maintenance_rate < 130:
        levels["zone"] = "?? ?梢?嚗?20~130%嚗?
        levels["risk"] = "擃?- ?航閫貊餈賜像隞?
        levels["action"] = "霅衣內嚗?撖?蝥?鞈???
    elif maintenance_rate < 160:
        levels["zone"] = "? 閫撖?嚗?30~160%嚗?
        levels["risk"] = "銝?- ????撠?蕭蝜?
        levels["action"] = "????"

        levels["zone"] = "? 摰?嚗? 160%嚗?
        levels["risk"] = "雿?- ???寧鋡怨翰鞈??"
        levels["action"] = "甇?虜??"
    
    return levels

```python
def calc_adr_premium(adr_price: float, fx_rate: float, ratio: float, tw_price: float) -> dict:
    """
    adr_price: ADR ?嗥?對?蝢?嚗?
    fx_rate: ?箇??舐?嚗?憒?31.5嚗?
    ratio: ADR 頧?瘥?嚗SM = 5, ADS = 2嚗?
    tw_price: ?箄?嗥?對??箏馳嚗?
    """
    implied_tw = adr_price * fx_rate / ratio
    premium = (implied_tw - tw_price) / tw_price * 100
    
    signal = ""
    if premium > 2:
        signal = "? 甇?滯?????箇??餃?賡???
    elif premium < -2:
        signal = "? 鞎滯?????箇??餃?賭???
    else:
        signal = "? 撠?皞Ｗ ???敶梢??"
    
    return {
        "implied_tw_price": round(implied_tw, 2),
        "premium_pct": round(premium, 2),
        "signal": signal
    }

# 蝭?
result = calc_adr_premium(
    adr_price=175.50,  # TSM ADR ?嗥
    fx_rate=31.8,      # ?嗆?舐?
    ratio=5,           # 1 ADR = 5 ?箇??餉
    tw_price=940.0     # ?箇??餅??
)
```

## ?? 璅???瘚?

```python
# 蝐Ⅳ?葉摨西?蝞?靘?def calc_ci_index(foreign_ratio, trust_ratio, dealer_ratio, major_1000_ratio):
    """
    foreign_ratio: 憭??%
    trust_ratio: ??閮???
    dealer_ratio: ?芰??%
    major_1000_ratio: ?撐憭扳?%
    """
    institutional = foreign_ratio + trust_ratio + dealer_ratio
    ci_index = institutional * 0.6 + major_1000_ratio * 0.4
    
    if ci_index >= 70:
        signal = "? 擃漲?葉 - 銝餃???
    elif ci_index >= 50:
        signal = "? 銝剖漲?葉 - 瘜犖撣?"
    else:
        signal = "? ? - ??銝餃?"
    
    return {"ci_index": round(ci_index, 2), "signal": signal}
```

## ?? 憭扳銵??菜葫璅∪?

### Prompt Atlas 蝑嚗??蝺???

```python
SENTIMENT_PROMPT = """
隢??誑銝??憿?撣??嚗?????-10 ??+10嚗?  +10 = 璆萄漲璅?嚗撥?憭?
    0 = 銝剜?  -10 = 璆萄漲?脰?嚗撥?蝛綽?

?芾??摮??詨?銝?亙??牧??
?啗?璅?嚗headline}
"""

def score_headlines(headlines: list) -> dict:
    scores = []
    for h in headlines:
        # ?澆 LLM 閰?
        score = llm_evaluate(SENTIMENT_PROMPT.format(headline=h))
        scores.append(score)
    
    avg_score = sum(scores) / len(scores)
    
    return {
        "average_sentiment": round(avg_score, 2),
        "signal": classify_sentiment(avg_score),
        "scored_headlines": list(zip(headlines, scores))
    }

def classify_sentiment(score: float) -> str:
    if score >= 7:   return "? 璆萄漲鞎芸帚嚗??征嚗?
    if score >= 4:   return "?? 璅?嚗??游?憭?"
    if score >= 1:   return "? 頛凝??"
    if score >= -1:  return "??銝剜?
    if score >= -4:  return "? 頛凝?征"
    if score >= -7:  return "?? ?脰?嚗??游?蝛綽?"
    return "? 璆萄漲??嚗???嚗?
```

## ??撌乩?瘚?Agentic Protocol嚗?
